# Sprite geometry measured on RGSS103J.dll under Wine (probes ph-scene-turnedvp, -bush, -srcrect, -geom2,
# -bushzoom and -srcrect2): turned sprites in viewports, the bush depth of turned and of zoomed sprites, and
# source rectangles that leave the bitmap. Decoded from the frames of the original runtime.
# Pattern bitmap 24x16, texel (x,y) = (8+10x, 8+15y, 200, 255); "u,v" = that texel at full opacity, "u,v*" = at
# opacity 127 over black (the faint rows of bush_depth), "." = nothing drawn (black).
# Per case: the parameters (positions are SCREEN coordinates here; viewport = [x, y, w, h, ox, oy] in screen
# coordinates), the number of drawn pixels, their bounding box [left, top, right, bottom) and three rows of it
# (first, middle, last), each from the left to the right edge of the bounding box.
# Case n of a topic was drawn in the cell of 160 by 120 pixels at (160 * (n % 4), 120 * (n / 4 % 4)), and the
# pixels were counted inside that cell.
# "model wrong" = pixels of the cell where the rule the measurement arrived at differs (always 0).

## topic turnedvp (probe ph-scene-turnedvp-probe.rb)
